R16 [Closing Balance]:
   G16 =SUM(G13:G15)
   H16 =SUM(H13:H15)

[?2004l[?2004h> with open('/workspace/variability.txt','w') as f:
[?2004l[?2004h>     for ws in wb.worksheets:
[?2004l[?2004h>         f.write(f'\n==== Variability {ws.title} ====\n')
[?2004l[?2004h>         for row in ws.iter_rows():
[?2004l[?2004h>             fs=[c for c in row if isinstance(c.value,str) and c.value.startswith('=') and not c.value.startswith('=TABLE')]
[?2004l[?2004h>             if len(fs)<2: continue
[?2004l[?2004h>             pats={}
[?2004l[?2004h>             for c in fs:
[?2004l[?2004h>                 t=ad.sub('R', c.value[1:])
[?2004l[?2004h>                 pats.setdefault(t,[]).append(c.coordinate)
[?2004l[?2004h>             if len(pats)>1:
[?2004l[?2004h>                 f.write(f'Row {row[0].row} patterns={len(pats)}\n')
[?2004l[?2004h>                 for c in fs:
[?2004l[?2004h>                     f.write(f'  {c.coordinate}: {c.value}\n')
[?2004l[?2004h> print('wrote variability.txt', sum(1 for _ in open('/workspace/variability.txt')))
[?2004l[?2004h> PY
